*Rule (phase 9, per NODE, EDGE and SEAM).* The subject's **dominant descriptor** is its live descriptor of highest `sev` (ties to the lowest `mode` id); `mode_key` = `(mode << 3) | sev` of that descriptor, 0 when none has `sev ≥ 1` (a u8, since `mode` ≤ 27 and `sev` ≤ 4; the boot assertion `GRAPH_BOOT_MODE_ID` refuses a build whose largest mode id exceeds 31, because `(mode << 3) | sev` would otherwise truncate silently — widening is a schema bump) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. On each run phase 9 computes the key from the run's state and compares it with the persisted `mode_key`. If they differ, then `mode_since_step` = `step_no` (0 when the new key is 0), `mode_key` = the new key, and, when `isolated_at_step` > 0, `isolated_at_step` = `step_no` too (so `grow_steps` is 0 and never negative). The key does **not** change when a closed valve drops `intensity_pm` to 0 (isolation changes the flow, not the mode or `sev`), so isolation only stamps `isolated_at_step` (LS-11). A SEAM's `mode_since_step` starts at its `built_step`. A cascade-forced mode (`forced_mode_next` applied in phase 0) changes the key at the next phase 9 like any other change. Because `mode_key` is persisted, a snapshot taken on any tick restores to the same key and no restamp occurs on load.

*Worked example (default tuning).* A coolant PIPE wears: `hp` falls below `HP_OK`, WORN sev 1 from step 1,000 (`warn_since_step` 1,000, key (10 << 3) | 1 = 81, `mode_since_step` 1,000). At step 1,500 `hp` falls below `LEAK_HP`: LEAK, say sev 2, key (1 << 3) | 2 = 10, `mode_since_step` 1,500, so `mode_steps` is 0 on the first LEAK run while `since_steps` is already 500. At step 1,620 the leak reaches sev 3 (key 11): `mode_since_step` 1,620, `mode_steps` 0. At step 1,700 the player closes the upstream valve: `intensity_pm` falls to 0, `isolated_at_step` 1,700, `grow_steps` = 1,700 − 1,620 = 80 and stays 80 at step 1,900 while `mode_steps` reads 280 (dullness keeps ageing, extent does not grow). The valve reopens at step 2,000: `isolated_at_step` 0, `grow_steps` = 2,000 − 1,620 = 380 — the one-time extent step bounded by the edge that Failure & Trail's FT-8 documents. A breach cascade restamps on its own sev rise, so the bent rib of Failure & Trail's ET-3 starts at its source with `grow_steps` 0.

*Edge rules.* **Non-dominant descriptors**: a subject with two live descriptors (a LEAK pipe that is also in the FREEZE pre-stage) carries one onset pair, for the dominant one; the other reads `mode_steps` = `grow_steps` = `since_steps` (the legacy reading) until it becomes dominant, because a per-mode array on every subject would cost a field per mode id on up to 12,288 subjects (Open Question 39). **Dormancy**: the section record gains a persisted `dormant_since_step`, stamped by M19 when the section goes dormant; on resume M19 advances `mode_since_step` and a non-zero `isolated_at_step` of every subject in the section by `step_no − dormant_since_step`, so the age counts only steps the section ran (Failure & Trail's reading); `warn_since_step`, `fail_since_step`, `built_step` and `patched_at_step` are advanced by the same amount (LS-17), so `since_steps` and `warn_elapsed` also count only the steps the section ran and Open Question 38 is closed *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. **Authored dead faults**: Derelict Authoring back-dates `mode_since_step` wherever it back-dates `fail_since_step`, sets `mode_key` to the fault's key and leaves `isolated_at_step` 0.

**LS-15 — Seams: a weld moves nothing.** Sections are instantiated into the one world graph when they exist (`instantiate_section`); ids come from the block's `next_*` counters in template order, so a weld **only adds edges** and never renumbers. A weld plan is plain data `{ section_a, section_b, pairs[]: (node_a, port_a, node_b, port_b, loop), openings[]: (comp_a, comp_b, area_max), L_seam, q }` validated by the pure query `can_join(plan)` with the closed reason set `SECTION_UNKNOWN`, `SECTION_NOT_LOADED` (dormant counts as not loaded), `SOCKET_OCCUPIED`, `PORT_INCOMPAT`, `PORT_ABSENT` (ABSENT, DESTROYED or SALVAGED endpoint), `SEAM_LIMIT` (> `MAX_SEAMS_PER_SECTION` 8), `ALREADY_JOINED`, `CAPACITY` (`MAX_GRAPH_NODES` 4096 / `MAX_GRAPH_EDGES` 8192), `POWER_CYCLE` (the cable pairs would close a cycle in POWER wiring — R12: islands are trees, cycles are rejected here and at data load, VS-A may lift it), `STABILITY` (D.12). It returns the fully resolved plan (slot indices, edge count) and informational `surge[]` per sub-edge (D.14), flagging an opening whose `|ΔP| > WELD_DP_WARN` 300 dkPa — **a warning, never a reject**; the warning is diegetic: the closed hatch's own `SEAL_STRAIN` descriptor (CREAK at the rim, rim frost when `|ΔT| > HATCH_DT_FROST_MC`; D.14, LS-14) and the corruptible gauge. `surge[]` reaches the player only through those tells and instruments — never as a number in a weld preview or any other UI; Assembly's preview may consume `surge[].warn` to pick a diegetic cue, not to print the value *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)*. `weld_join` then only appends the pre-validated edges, writes the SeamRecord, sets every sub-edge **disconnected** (R9: opening `area` 0, pipe and duct valves closed, cable breaker open), bumps `topology_version` and marks `T` + `S/<seam_id>` + `SEC/<a>` + `SEC/<b>`; nothing in it can fail, so CR-9's infallible apply holds. Connecting is then three lit verbs (`set_opening_area`, `set_valve`, `set_breaker`), each with its own surge read-out. The seam weeps through `A_seam_m` from the step it exists (D.2, D.14) until wear-in or a seam PATCH (M7; V11 SEAL_SEAM is the VS-A form) — the weld's own P1 tell. **Structural half, returns, seam read** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from modular-ship-assembly, ship-section-and-layout-data-model)*: `can_join_static(plan)` is published as a pure query returning the first failing reason of the nine structural reasons in LS-15 order — `SECTION_UNKNOWN`, `SECTION_NOT_LOADED`, `SOCKET_OCCUPIED`, `PORT_INCOMPAT`, `SEAM_LIMIT`, `ALREADY_JOINED`, `CAPACITY`, `POWER_CYCLE`, `STABILITY` — reading no node `hp`, `state`, `forced_mode` or `served_pm` and computing no `surge[]`; `can_join` adds `PORT_ABSENT` (a node-state read) at its LS-15 position and the `surge[]` read-out. `weld_join` returns `{seam_id, opening_ids[]}`; `seam_view(seam_id)` → `{state, sub_edges[{kind, id}], leak_bp, opening_ids[], cut_sides[]}` is pure *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, routed from modular-ship-assembly D54 / ASK G10)*: `cut_sides` is empty unless the seam is `CUT` with boundary RAGGED, and then holds one entry per side, `{section_id, cut_holes[{cid, a_hole}], uncapped_open_ends}`, where `cid` is the compartment carrying that side's A_CUT virtual hole (one per former opening), `a_hole` is the hole's CURRENT D.2 area (24 at `hp` 2500; 0 once a Vertical Slice plate install or repair closes it) and `uncapped_open_ends` is the count of that side's fluid ports still `open_end`. It is derived from the hull-stub records and port flags already in `graph.state` (no new persisted field; the SeamRecord tombstone keeps the cut hole ids). The Graph has no keep / leave notion: it returns BOTH sides keyed by `section_id`, and Assembly picks the keep sides from Layout's `keep_sections`. A welded section keeps its `kind` and `rate_pm` (LS-17, LS-25): M2 does not promote a DERELICT section, so a cut restores nothing. An as-welded seam is **not quiet**: it moves nothing between the sections, but it weeps (HISS at the seam) from its first run until wear-in brings `leak_bp` to 0 at period 100 or V1 patches it — the weep tapers **linearly with `leak_bp`** to a cliff at 0 (D.14), not a function of `q`. **Intended seam clock** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: a default weld (`q` 850, `L_seam` 3) is tight at period 100 = 16.7 min; the weld-side bay loses about 20 % of its gas over that time if nothing compensates: its total pressure never leaves NORMAL (D.15 SEAM-WEEP), but with its vent isolated its O2 band enters THIN at about step 4,325 (7.2 min) and stays there until the vent runs or the seam is sealed — a deliberate sev-1 tell of the unvented bay, absent with the vent live; V1 PATCH on the seam ends the weep at once (M7 sets `leak_bp` to 0 on a SEAM) and a running vent makes the gas up. The weep carries no LEAK wear (D.9) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. *Alternative considered:* opening the seam at weld with a hard reject above a `ΔP` limit (SD OQ-13) — rejected (R9): it created a vacuum-section soft-lock and a loop-surge leak; the disconnected start removes both and makes every connection a readable choice.

**LS-16 — Cuts, only along seams.** `cut_detach(seam_id, boundary_mode)` is atomic in one P2 apply. `SEALED` (cut at a bulkhead or hatch): the seam's OPENINGs are removed, its loop sub-edges tombstoned, both sides' fluid ports `capped` — nothing leaks. `RAGGED` (cut through the weld): for each former opening a virtual hole of `A_CUT` 24 is attached to each side's compartment as a hull stub with `hp` 2500 (sev 4 — D.4 gives sev 4 at `hp` ≤ 2500 — BREACH, exogenous — no gate) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling G4)*, and each side's fluid ports become `open_end` (SEVERED) leaking at `OPEN_END_RATE_PM` (500 ‰ of port cap per step) until `cap_port`. **The two RAGGED hazards are independent** *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix, routed from modular-ship-assembly D54)*: `cap_port` (M12) closes a fluid port's hiss only and never changes the hull hole. The A_CUT hull stub (`hp` 2500, `a_hole` 24) has no subject in the MVP: no verb patches, caps or repairs it, so the cut room vents to its steady state, `band_p` VACUUM, and stays a vacuum until a Vertical Slice plate install closes the hole (the same "room is lost" rule as a DESTROYED hull segment). With `n` holes of area A_CUT on a room of volume `V`: `k = G_OPEN × A_CUT × n / (V × GAS_SCALE)`; the room reaches DECOMP (`P` < 400) in `ceil(ln(1000/400)/k)` steps and VACUUM (`P` < 100) in `ceil(ln(1000/100)/k)` steps, `P0` 1000 dkPa nominal. `V` 20, `n` 1: `k` = 2 × 24 / 20000 = 0.0024; DECOMP `ceil(381.8)` = 382 steps = 2,292 ticks (38 s); VACUUM `ceil(959.4)` = 960 steps = 5,760 ticks (96 s). `V` 30, `n` 1: `k` = 0.0016; VACUUM `ceil(1439.1)` = 1,440 steps = 8,640 ticks. A room open to a larger connected volume vents more slowly (`k` uses the connected volume). Guarantees: no inventory lives in edges, so removing them creates or destroys nothing; power has no inventory; the current step's exchange across the seam is simply not computed; one notification batch with `T`, `S/<seam_id>`, affected `C/<id>`; the next run re-derives components. Cutting through a compartment is not a Graph operation and is not allowed (R10): a compartment belongs to one section and a cut never splits gas by a volume ratio. `discard_section(section_id)` (jettison, Web Map unload in VS-A) tombstones a section's slots; mass in it leaves the world and is added to the counters as `discarded_*` so the invariant still closes; ids are never reused.

**LS-17 — Sections and dormancy.** Every node, edge and compartment carries `section_id`; a section record holds `kind` (HOME / DERELICT), `dormant`, `rate_pm` (LS-25), `stress_until_step`, the three conservation counters and `discarded_*`. A section is **dormant** when its derelict is not loaded (zoomed out, left behind): its slots are skipped by every phase, its atmosphere and failures stand still, its state is persisted and hashed unchanged, and it cannot be welded (`SECTION_NOT_LOADED`). The flag is written only by M19 `set_section_dormant` (Web Map & Transit at load/unload) and read through the pure `section(section_id)` → `{kind, dormant, rate_pm, stress_active}` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from web-map-and-transit)*. The section record also carries `dormant_since_step` (persisted; stamped when `dormant` is set); on load M19 also advances, by `step_no − dormant_since_step`, every subject's `mode_since_step` and non-zero `isolated_at_step` (GA-T, LS-14: a trail's age counts only the steps the section ran) **and** `warn_since_step` (while `has_warn`), `fail_since_step` (of every FAILED or DESTROYED subject) and `patched_at_step` (of every patched subject, `hp_cap` < `HP_FULL`), and every SeamRecord's `built_step` and `mode_since_step`, so the fairness gate never counts as served a warning the player never witnessed and a patch's decay clock does not run while the section sleeps; `stress_until_step` is deliberately not advanced (the breach rhythm's stress lapses while a section sleeps); then it sets `dormant_since_step` to 0 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. Loading it clears `dormant` and it resumes from its last state with no fast-forward (R13; SD OQ-2 resolved: cheapest, and a derelict that "does not degrade on its own" is the boarding rhythm GD asks for). VS-A (ADR-LSG-04): on unload the partition is serialised into Web Map's deltas and the slots are compacted away by an explicit order-preserving compaction mutator; reload re-creates them with **new** ids. Quiescence sleeping of islands (D.16) is the VS-A lever on top of dormancy, with its flag persisted so a restore resumes with the same awake set.

**LS-18 — Published mutators (M1–M19), two classes.** All are callable only inside a kernel-issued `WriteScope` in P2/P3, every signature takes the scope token first, and every one marks its dirty keys explicitly (packed-array element writes run no setter). **Class A** is command-backed: paired with a pure `check_*` used by the owner command's `validate`; `apply` is infallible once the check passed. **Class B** is the unconditional channel (kernel CR-9 path (a)): never rejects, clamps, and treats an unknown or stale id as a no-op returning 0/false. The Graph registers **no commands of its own**; the domain commands live with Assembly, Interaction, Suit, Emitter State and Horror (kernel H2).

| # | Mutator | Args | Validation / clamp | Caller | Class |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| M1 | `instantiate_section` → `{node_base, edge_base, comp_base}` | `section_id, template_id, kind` | id unused; `CAPACITY`; template ids unique; POWER acyclic; advances `next_section_id` to `max(section_id) + 1`; template `fail_since_step` / `warn_since_step` may be ≤ 0 (a back-dated dried fault, e.g. ≤ −`DRIED_AFTER_STEPS`), and a template that supplies a `warn_since_step` of any value sets `has_warn`, so a back-dated warning is a live, long-served warning and not a missing one *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; a VENT or LOAD may be authored bare (no `def_id`, no hardpoint) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from ship-section-and-layout-data-model, derelict-authoring-handmade-kits)* | Layout / derelict build, home-ship setup | A |
| M2 | `weld_join` → `{seam_id, opening_ids[]}` | `plan` | `can_join` OK | Assembly (`weld`) | A |
| M3 | `cut_detach` | `seam_id, boundary_mode` | seam exists and ACTIVE | Assembly (`seam_cut`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from modular-ship-assembly)* | A |
| M4 | `discard_section` | `section_id` | exists; no ACTIVE seam | Web Map only (Assembly never discards) | A |
| M5 | `install_module` | `slot_node_id, def_id, hp_start, hp_cap = HP_FULL, patched_at_step = 0` | node ABSENT or SALVAGED (else `SLOT_OCCUPIED`); ports match the slot's hardpoint (else `PORT_INCOMPAT`); a patched module reinstalls capped and keeps its absolute `patched_at_step` decay clock; a store installs empty *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from item-and-module-database, modular-ship-assembly)* | Assembly (`install_module`); the Item DB calls no mutator | A |
| M6 | `salvage_node` → `SalvageRecord{def_id, hp, state, hp_cap, patched_at_step}` | `node_id` | node not ABSENT/SALVAGED; not a COMPARTMENT/EXTERIOR/HULL_SEGMENT; a node with no `def_id` (bare authored VENT/LOAD) is refused `NOT_SALVAGEABLE`; a store's remaining stock leaves the world into the section's `discarded_*` counter at the apply *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from item-and-module-database)* | Interaction (`begin_cut`); the yield is the Item DB's `salvage_yield` (registry: `salvage_yield`), deterministic at MVP | A |
| M7 | `repair_step` | `subject_kind, id, delta_bp, kind ∈ {PATCH, FULL}` | not DESTROYED (`NOT_REPAIRABLE`); `delta` clamped by `hp_cap`; PATCH sets `hp_cap = min(hp_cap, PATCH_CAP_BP)` and `patched_at_step`; FULL restores `hp_cap = HP_FULL` (Item DB part named by `repair_part_for(subject)`); PATCH on `hp ≥ PATCH_CAP_BP` is refused `ABOVE_PATCH_CAP` — `hp` is never clamped down, Interaction offers only FULL there; FULL on `subject_kind` SEAM 4 is not offered at MVP (`repair_part_for` NONE); **PATCH on a SEAM (`subject_kind` 4) is exempt from `ABOVE_PATCH_CAP`** — a seam's `hp` is its weld quality (`q` × 10), not a wear state — and sets the SeamRecord's `leak_bp` to 0 (the weep ends at once); at `hp ≥ PATCH_CAP_BP` it changes no other field (no `hp_cap`, no `patched_at_step`, so the seal does not decay), below the cap the ordinary patch also applies *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from item-and-module-database, interaction-system)* | Interaction (`begin_patch` channel steps; each step re-runs `check_repair_step` in the channel hook and a failing check skips that step's apply, IA-12) | A |
| M8 | `set_valve` | `edge_id, open` | edge kind VALVE or seam PIPE/DUCT sub-edge | Interaction | A |
| M9 | `set_breaker` | `node_id or seam cable id, closed` | CABLE_JUNCTION or seam CABLE sub-edge; closing sets `energised_at_step` on the branch's loads (inrush) | Interaction | A |
| M10 | `set_setpoint` | `node_id, key ∈ {vent_pp, pump_throttle}, value` | key legal for kind; clamped to range | Interaction | A |
| M11 | `reset_node` | `node_id` | clears SCRAM / FREEZE only if the clear condition holds; otherwise result `BLOCKED` (not a command reject: the physical reason is the answer) | Interaction | A |
| M12 | `cap_port` | `node_id, port_index` | port is `open_end` | Interaction | A |
| M13 | `set_opening_area` | `edge_id, area` | OPENING exists; `area ≤ area_max`; door motor power is Interaction's precondition, not the Graph's (the Graph has none, a SYSTEM close included) | Interaction *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from modular-ship-assembly, interaction-system)* | A |
| M14 | `set_load_request` | `node_id, req_w` | LOAD; clamped 0..`req_max`; queued in the persisted `pending_load` and applied at the next phase 0 (LS-21) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | Emitter State (lamps), Interaction (machines) | B |
| M15 | `draw_air` | `compartment_id, crew_id, o2_mgu` (returns nothing; queued) | queued to the next run's phase 0 (the queue is persisted in `graph.state`, LS-21, so a snapshot between hook ticks loses no breath) and applied there in (compartment, crew) order against the running balance, clamped to `n_o2`; the grant is published as `draw_grant(crew)` → `{requested, granted}` until the following run *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from suit-and-equipment)* | Suit (`after: graph.propagate.atmo`; a declared Class B call) | B |
| M16 | `withdraw_store` | `node_id, amount` → granted | clamped to stock; `amount` in the store's own unit (mGU for O2_TANK, mL for FUEL_TANK and RESERVOIR, dJ for BATTERY — legal on a BATTERY); the amount leaves the world into the section's `withdrawn_*` counter so the conservation sum closes; a stock debit is not a power draw and never appears in `power_use` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from suit-and-equipment, web-map-and-transit)* | Suit (a declared Class B call), Interaction (refill, refuel), Web Map & Transit (hop fuel from the home FUEL_TANK) | B |
| M17 | `deposit_trace` (footprint channel) | `kind ∈ {CHILL, BURN, GOUGE, RUPTURE, STRESS}, subject_id, magnitude` | per-kind cap: CHILL/BURN `≤ FOOTPRINT_MAX_Q` 20000 dJ into a compartment; GOUGE `≤ FOOTPRINT_MAX_GOUGE_BP` 2500 off a HULL_SEGMENT only at MVP, sets `fatigued` (a GOUGE on a duct or cable is dropped and counted in `graph_footprints_dropped`; duct/cable GOUGE is VS-A, matching Horror Entities' restriction) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from failure-and-trail-system, horror-entities)*; RUPTURE sets a hull/duct/pipe `hp = min(hp, RUPTURE_HP)` and starts LEAK/BREACH (exogenous); STRESS sets the section's `stress_until_step = step_no + STRESS_HOLD_STEPS` and `stress_pm = BREACH_PROPAGATION_MULT` (D.9, LS-25; Attention passes that constant as `magnitude`, any other value is ignored); calls are queued in the persisted `pending_trace` (LS-21; a call past the 16th of a run is dropped at enqueue, so the queue is bounded) and drained at the next run's phase 0, and at most `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP` 16 per run **across all callers and subjects** are honoured — the first 16 by arrival (kernel tick, then call order), applied in ascending subject id — the rest counted and dropped *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from attention-system, horror-entities)* | Horror Entities (CHILL, BURN, GOUGE, RUPTURE), Attention (STRESS, the only STRESS caller, at the breach-rhythm transition) | B |
| M18 | `apply_damage` | `subject_kind, id, hp_bp, cause ∈ {ENDOGENOUS, EXOGENOUS}` | clamp `hp ≥ 0`; EXOGENOUS bypasses the gate | environment and impact sources (integer payloads only — never physics read inside a hook); not Assembly, not Horror *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from modular-ship-assembly, horror-entities)* | B |
| M19 | `set_section_dormant` | `section_id, dormant` | `SECTION_UNKNOWN`; `NO_CHANGE` (already in that state); `SEAM_ACTIVE` (an ACTIVE seam joins it to a loaded section); the avatar-present check is the caller's `validate` (Web Map reads Body) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from web-map-and-transit)*; sets `dormant_since_step` on unload and advances the section's absolute step stamps (`mode_since_step`, `isolated_at_step`, `warn_since_step`, `fail_since_step`, `patched_at_step`, a seam's `built_step`) by the dormant duration on load (LS-17) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | Web Map & Transit (load/unload) | A |

**Check names, verb map, positions** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from interaction-system, modular-ship-assembly)*. Each Class A mutator has a published pure check: `check_instantiate_section`, `can_join` (M2), `check_cut_detach`, `check_discard_section`, `check_install_module` (`SLOT_OCCUPIED`, `PORT_INCOMPAT`), `check_salvage_node`, `check_repair_step`, `check_set_valve`, `check_set_breaker`, `check_set_setpoint`, `check_reset_node` (domain only — `BLOCKED` is apply's physical result), `check_cap_port`, `check_set_opening_area`, `check_set_section_dormant`. The M8/M9 kind verdict is direction-neutral (either endpoint order of a seam sub-edge passes). V6 emergency vent is M13 `set_opening_area` to `area_max` on an exterior OPENING (one side EXTERIOR, node 0); no `vent` kind flag exists. No Graph phase writes `area` or `door_state`: only M13 does, on a template door or a mated hatch; virtual openings (LS-5) have no leaf. A PUMP is switched off by M10 `pump_throttle` 0; a REACTOR has no switch-off command at MVP — its `out_w` reaches 0 only by SCRAM, fuel starve or failure. **V12 hand crank** is M11 `reset_node` and nothing else (Interaction IA-27 reads it so): the Graph has no crank source, no stored-energy restart helper and no bounded generator, and `BLOCKED(STILL_HOT)` / `BLOCKED(NO_FUEL)` are its whole physical answer *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.

**LS-19 — `read_view` is the one true channel.** Pure, side-effect free, callable from `validate`, P3 and presentation; it may call `ensure_derived()` (a rebuild of non-hashed caches), which keeps `validate`'s hash-unchanged guarantee. **`ensure_derived()` is eager** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: every structural mutator (M1–M6, M19) and `post_restore` finishes by rebuilding the derived caches, so a read never triggers a rebuild in an arbitrary presentation frame (the weld-preview drag calls pure `can_join` against caches that are already derived) and the lazy call remains only as a no-op guard. **Return shapes on the hot path** are typed scalar getters (`compartment_p(cid)`, `compartment_t(cid)`, `node_hp(id)`, `served_pm(id)`, …) or one reusable view object per kind that the caller must read before its next call; the Dictionary-shaped returns listed below are for tests, the Debug Overlay and cold paths. `failures(filter)` returns index slices of the per-step columnar descriptor store (preallocated parallel packed arrays, one slot per live descriptor) and allocates nothing. Queries: `compartment(cid)` → `{V, n_o2, n_inert, P, p_o2, o2_pm, T, band_o2, band_p, band_t, pressure_trend, fuel_puddle_ml, status}`; `compartment_at(room_ref)`; `node(id)` / `edge(id)` → `{state, hp, hp_cap, forced_mode, served_pm, stock, flow, sev, warn_elapsed}`; `loop_component(loop, any_node)` → `{pool, cap, supply, demand, sat_by_tier[5], members}`; `failures(filter)` → sorted `FailureDescriptor[]`; `power_use(section_id)` → `{draw_w, delta_w_last5, lamps_lit_w, noisy_w}` (Attention's stimulus); `served_pm(node_id)` (lamp brightness = base × served / 1000); `can_join(plan)`; `neighbors(cid)`, `nodes_in(cid)`, `section_of(id)`; `alarm_sections()` → ascending, duplicate-free `section_id`s owning an `alarm_eligible` descriptor in the last committed run (LS-13 (c)) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; `summary()`; `true_value(kind, id)` — the unfiltered value, which the Perception Filter is the only non-diagnostic reader allowed to wrap; `block_hash()`. There is no "perceived" variant anywhere in the Graph.

**LS-19 additions** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from emitter-state-and-light-exposure-query, in-world-instruments-and-inspect-view, attention-system, ship-section-and-layout-data-model, adaptive-audio-and-tells, ship-body-and-interior-physics, save-load-and-versioned-format, debug-overlay-true-values)*. `node(id)` also returns `stock_pm` (`⌊1000 × stock / capacity⌋`, capacity = the def's `cap_ml`, `BATT_CELL` or the O2_TANK template maximum), `switch_pos` (a CABLE_JUNCTION's `breaker_closed`; a VALVE edge's `open` through `edge(id)`), the request fields `req_w` / `throttle_pm` / `out_w`, and `run_pm` (0..1000): 0 when the requested load is 0 (LOAD `req_w` 0, SCRUBBER `req_w` 0, PUMP `throttle_pm` 0, REACTOR `out_w` 0) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*, otherwise `served_pm` for a consumer (a SCRUBBER's `run_pm` is `served_pm` while its request is above 0; a scrubber throttled to free tank space is still running and is not dimmed by the throttle, and its O2 output is D.5's concern, not `run_pm`) — except a HEATER, whose `run_pm` is `⌊duty_t × served_pm / 1000⌋` (0 while its thermostat is satisfied, although its `req_w` stays 500) — and `⌊1000 × out_w / out_w_rated⌋` for a REACTOR; this is also the D.9 `duty_pm`, so duty wear stops on a switched-off pump, a satisfied heater and a reactor at `out_w` 0 (the first draft keyed duty wear to `served_pm`, which reads 1000 for a machine switched off on a met tier) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. `opening(edge_id)` → `{area, area_max, door_state}` for stored OPENINGs (`door_state` derived from `area`: CLOSED at 0, OPEN at `area_max`, PARTIAL between). `open_ends(node_id)` → the port indices with `open_end`. `stress_active(section_id)` = `step_no < stress_until_step` (the STRESS armed state). `draw_grant(crew_id)` → `{requested, granted}` is the M15 grant applied at the last phase 0 (the table is persisted, LS-21, so a restore between hook ticks returns the same grant) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. `hull_segments_of(compartment_id)` lists a room's hull segments (the Graph has no pixel geometry; a nearest-point query is Layout's). `soonest_warning_remaining(loaded_only)` → ticks `(W_MIN_STEPS − warn_elapsed) × GRAPH_STEP_TICKS`, minimum over live descriptors whose gate is still closed, skipping dormant sections when `loaded_only` (a dormant section freezes `warn_since_step`, so its descriptors would never close). `compartment_at` for an actor whose `actor_section` is NONE returns the exterior's atmosphere (`n_o2` 0, no pressure), never the last compartment's. `true_value(kind, id)` kinds are the In-World Instruments IW-9 set — `p_o2`, `P`, `T` (compartment), `stock_pm` (node), `supply`, `demand` (POWER component) — plus `hp` for diagnostics. `loop_component(POWER, node)`: `supply` = `S + D` (D.6 `avail`, W this step), `demand` = `Σ R_k` (requested W, inrush included); Instruments' `load = ⌊1000 × demand / max(1, supply)⌋` is its own reading, not `ρ`, and reads no `cap`. `power_use(section_id)`, all in W per step for the section's consumers, recomputed in phase 2: `draw_w` = Σ served W (all tiers, lamps and inrush included, battery charge excluded); `delta_w_last5` = `draw_w` now − `draw_w` five runs ago (signed; lamps included — D.6 Example C's 1200 W contains its 300 W of lamps; `post_restore` seeds the window flat, so it reads 0 after a load); `lamps_lit_w` = Σ served W of LOAD subtype LAMP, including any LAMP node such as `bcn_main` (the Graph does not know a beacon, so its 20 W step is in `delta_w_last5` too); `noisy_w` = Σ served W of SCRUBBER, PUMP, HEATER and DOOR_MOTOR nodes (lamps excluded). `o2_pm`, `n_o2` and `n_inert` have no dirty-key quantum of their own: they ride the `C/<cid>` key fired by the `P` (10 dkPa) and `p_o2` (5 dkPa) buckets and stay exact in `read_view`.

**LS-20 — Dirty keys confirm `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS`.** Keys are ints `(KIND << 32) | id` with KIND ∈ {COMP 1, BAND 2, NODE 3, EDGE 4, DESC 5, LOOP 6, SEAM 7, TOPO 8, SECTION 9} (written in this document by their family names `C/<cid>`, `B/<cid>`, `N/<id>`, `F/<id>`, `L/<loop>/<anchor>`, `S/<seam_id>`, `T`, `SEC/<id>`); no String is built per key per run. Rules: continuous values never dirty per step — a key fires only when `⌊value / quantum⌋` changes bucket (quanta: `T` 1000 mC, `P` 10 dkPa, `p_o2` 5 dkPa, store 20 ‰, intensity 100 ‰, `hp` 250 bp); one `C/<cid>` per compartment per step, not one per field, fired when the `T`, `P` **or** `p_o2` bucket changed (three triggers, not `P` alone) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; structural change uses `T` + `SEC/<id>`, never per-node keys; last-emitted buckets are derived and `post_restore` seeds them so a restore yields one `ALL` and then silence; Instruments read values only through `perceived` and the honest needle moves on LS-20 quantum crossings as a one-step ramp (In-World Instruments IW-11); Failure & Trail polls once per Graph step and reads a per-step cache every frame (FT-15); Adaptive Audio reads per Graph step — keys wake discrete presentation only (trail spawn/despawn, loop on/off) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from in-world-instruments-and-inspect-view, failure-and-trail-system)*. D.10 bounds the worst designed cascade at 38 (MVP) and 135 (VS-A) keys, so **`MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256 stays as registered**; the Graph confirms it and asks that it never fall below 128 (MVP's worst case is 38; VS-A's 135 already exceeds 128) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. *Alternative considered:* SD's string-keyed families — the semantics are identical; the int encoding is chosen for the per-run allocation cost at VS-A (ADR-LSG-06) [UNV that the kernel's key type is Variant; if it is String, the family strings are the encoding and nothing else changes].

**LS-21 — Serialization, hash, summary, interchange.** *Persisted (canonical):* `schema_version`, `next_node_id`, `next_edge_id`, `next_seam_id`, `next_section_id`, `last_step_no` and the per-hook cursors `last_step_power`, `last_step_atmo`, `last_step_failure` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; the `def_table` CSR pair (`def_off`, `def_utf8`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; nodes and edges with every field of LS-3/LS-5 including tombstones; SeamRecords; sections with `dormant`, `dormant_since_step` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*, `rate_pm`, `stress_pm`, `stress_until_step`, `vented_mgu`, `radiated_dj`, `spilled_cool_ml`, `produced_mgu`, `breathed_mgu`, `produced_dj`, `burned_uml`, `spilled_heat_mdj` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, `discarded_*`, `withdrawn_*` (M16, per medium) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from web-map-and-transit)*; compartments `layout_room_ref`, `n_o2`, `n_inert`, `T`, `t_rem`, `fuel_puddle_ml` and the hysteresis memory `band_o2`, `band_p`, `band_t`, `p_prev` (the previous run's `P`), and the warn trackers `warn_since_o2`, `warn_since_p`, `warn_since_t` (i32 each) and `warn_bits` (u8) — canonical order after `p_prev` (+13 bytes per compartment, 65 for the MVP five) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*; every stored OPENING's `strain_gas` and `strain_heat` live bits (SEAL_STRAIN hysteresis, D.14); **and the three input queues** — `pending_draw` (compartment, crew, mGU), `pending_load` (node, `req_w`) and `pending_trace` (kind, subject, magnitude), each in arrival order and bounded (draw ≤ `MAX_CREW` × compartments, load ≤ 64, trace ≤ `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP`), plus the last applied `draw_grant` table. A snapshot can be taken on any tick, so a queue that held a breath, a trace or a load change between two hook ticks would otherwise be lost on restore and the restored run would diverge from the uninterrupted one *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. *Derived, never in a snapshot:* `V`, `exposed_tiles` (from Layout), components, trees, `path_cap`, degrees, id → slot maps, `served_pm`, descriptors, `P`/`p_o2`/`o2_pm`, last-emitted buckets, the cached hash. Rule: anything the next step depends on that is not re-derivable from persisted fields is persisted (`t_rem`, `tc_rem`, `fuel_rem`, `out_w`, `forced_mode_next`, `warn_since_step` with its `has_warn` bit, `thaw_until_step` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, the GA-T fields `mode_since_step`, `mode_key` and `isolated_at_step`, `dormant_since_step` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*, the three `pending_*` queues, the band and SEAL_STRAIN memory, the compartment warn trackers `warn_since_o2/p/t` and `warn_bits` *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*); `served_pm` is not, because phase 2 recomputes it before use. `canonical_bytes()` = the persisted fields in a fixed documented order, each as `[u32 tag][u32 length][field.to_byte_array()]`, raw slot arrays with tombstones — native memcpy, no interpreted loop. **`block_hash()`** is published: cached, recomputed only when the block was written since the last hash, H64 over `canonical_bytes()` (H64 = the first 8 bytes of the SHA-256 that native `HashingContext` returns, read as a little-endian u64 — a truncated hash, never a hand-rolled 64-bit mix) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; the kernel's `state_hash` is registered as `state_hash(scope, T) = H64( T || world_seed || concat over b in scope sorted by block_id of (len(block_id) || block_id || schema_version || len(canonical_bytes(b)) || block_hash(b)) )` with `block_hash(b) = H64(canonical_bytes(b))` cached per block and invalidated by the dirty bit, and the Graph's `canonical_bytes()` and `block_hash()` feed it unchanged *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass after design review)*. The one-level Merkle form (`state_hash` over per-block cached hashes, H64 via native `HashingContext` or S0-d's winner) is **kernel amendment K-1, routed to the kernel owner as an ADR candidate** and not a rule of this GDD (R5). **SHARED_SUMMARY** (VS-B): per compartment `T_dC` (int16, 0.1 °C), `P` (u16), `p_o2` (u16), three bands (3 bits each); per node/edge `state` (3 bits) + `sev` (3 bits) + `served_q` (0..15); per loop component `pool_q`; active descriptors compact `(id, mode, sev, alarm_eligible, intensity bucket = intensity_pm / 100, since_steps, mode_steps, grow_steps, each saturating at 2^24 − 1)` — 7 bytes for the first six fields plus 3 bytes for each of the two GA-T ages, 13 bytes each, from which a client derives `fail_since_step` and the trail's own age so two MIRROR crewmates see equal trail extent, dullness and density (CR-10, VS-B) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from failure-and-trail-system; GA-T fields added 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; seam list; about 0.4 KB at MVP, deltas ride the dirty keys; clients never receive `t_rem`, queues or RNG inputs — they receive truth, quantised. **Interchange (S-1, routed to Save/Load):** every Graph value stays below 2^53; the canonical form is packed int arrays; any JSON text form must carry the raw canonical bytes as base64 (`Marshalls.raw_to_base64`); `var_to_bytes` is not used — the `allow_objects` argument of the first draft is Godot 3-era and does not exist in 4.x, and Variant framing would put engine-version headers inside the hashed form *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; never a `Resource` for saves (R6; Godot JSON parses numbers as float — TD, sourced). Restore is two-phase (decode and validate into a scratch state, then swap); `restore(data, stored_version)` supports exactly version 1 in MVP, any other version is a reported failure, and every failure is a named `GRAPH_RESTORE_*` reason with the live state untouched (Section E). Type ids are append-only forever — the only forward-compatibility promise v1 makes.

**LS-22 — Falsifiability: perception-agnostic by construction.** No mutator argument carries a believed state; every argument is an id or a physical magnitude; the Graph never reads `PERCEPTION_OUT` (kernel CR-12) and `read_view` returns true values only, so a hallucinated "fixed" pipe (the Filter shows OK while `hp` = 2000) cannot change a Graph value and a hallucinated repair performs the real `repair_step` increments against the true `hp` — it costs time, not truth. Every gauge quantity has **one physical witness driven by the same Graph value**, so a lying instrument can be falsified by the world:

| Gauge quantity | Physical witness (same value) | Channel (LS-14) *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)* |
|---|---|---|
| O2 pressure / fraction | `ATMO_THIN` band: breath audio and Suit's walk-only state (SE-27; no vignette) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from suit-and-equipment, sanity-and-hallucination-rules, perception-filter)*; scrubber hum via `served_pm` | consequence (Suit body cues, HUM_DROP at the scrubber) |
| Temperature | `ATMO_COLD`/`ATMO_HOT`: frost, breath fog, shiver, shimmer | consequence (trail VFX, Suit body cues) |
| Pressure | `AIRFLOW` draught and dust at openings, pull toward the hole *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from suit-and-equipment)* | consequence (draught, Suit body cues) |
| Pressure across a closed hatch | `SEAL_STRAIN` (D.14): CREAK at the rim, bowed hatch, rim frost when `|ΔT|` > `HATCH_DT_FROST_MC`; the `AIRFLOW` draught the moment it opens | consequence (acoustic class at the anchor, trail VFX) |
| Power | lamp brightness `served_pm`, hum pitch, relay clunk on STARVE | consequence (lamp brightness, HUM_DROP, CLANK); the breaker panel's own lamp is module-body |
| Coolant | pipe frost and drip descriptors, radiator glow, pump cavitation TICK | consequence (DRIP, frost trail, TICK); the radiator's glow sprite is module-body |
| Hull | hiss, debris stream, frost along the hole, groan TICK with `since_steps` | consequence (HISS, WIND, trail VFX, TICK) |
| Fuel | sight-glass is Instruments; the puddle `PUDDLE` descriptor and drip are the witness | sight-glass module-body (corruptible); PUDDLE and DRIP consequence |

A WORN descriptor has no row: it is module-body and witnesses nothing. The witness of a worn machine is its falling output through D.4 (`out_scr`, `circ`, lamp brightness via `served_pm`) and, once a hazard mode takes over, that mode's consequence-channel descriptor. A PATCH_WEEP descriptor is on the consequence channel and witnesses the patch itself (`hp_cap < HP_FULL`): the Filter may repaint a patched module as whole, but may neither remove the weep nor add one to a sound module *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.

The headless test: set Perception output to "all OK" for a degraded fixture and assert `state_hash` of `graph.state` at tick N is identical with and without the Perception block's content (the kernel's own `PERCEPTION_OUT` pattern).

**LS-23 — Patch, repair, decay.** `repair_step(PATCH)` raises `hp` by its delta up to `hp_cap = min(hp_cap, PATCH_CAP_BP 6000)` and records `patched_at_step`; a patched subject is therefore permanently DEGRADED at sev 1 (a patch weeps: its tell is the consequence-channel PATCH_WEEP of LS-11, not WORN, so a hallucinated "fixed" module cannot hide a failing patch) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* — it works at `eff` 750 and the leak mode is cleared because `hp ≥ LEAK_HP`. After `PATCH_DECAY_STEPS` (2400 = `PATCH_DECAY_S` 240 × 10) the patch decays at `PATCH_DECAY_BP_PER_100` per wear period and re-leaks when `hp` drops below `LEAK_HP` again; the re-leak is endogenous and gated, and the gate is open because the sev-1 PATCH_WEEP descriptor has been live since the patch (R15). `repair_step(FULL)` requires the Item DB part named by `repair_part_for(subject)` (edges have no `def_id`) and restores `hp_cap = HP_FULL`; a PATCH consumes no item *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from item-and-module-database)*. DESTROYED is never repairable; the path is salvage → install. Nothing in the Graph rewards repair: no progression value is written anywhere (brief: no player progression from the Graph).

**LS-24 — Invariants, FAULTED tiers, bounded work.** Two tiers, and every invariant in this GDD belongs to one. *Structural (hard):* edge endpoint slot out of range or pointing at a tombstone; array lengths disagree; ids not strictly ascending; `next_id ≤ max id`; CSR inconsistent with edges; a seam sub-edge whose SeamRecord is missing — checked after every topology mutator in debug, in `post_restore` in all builds, every run in debug; a violation is a kernel step-invariant failure → FAULTED (dev halts; release returns to the menu; no autosave). Every check is raised through `GraphFault.raise(code, subject)`, which records the code, returns the failure to the kernel and, in debug, also halts; no invariant relies on GDScript `assert`, which is stripped from release builds and cannot be caught *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. *Numeric (soft):* an amount below 0, a field above its documented max, conservation drift ≠ 0, `ΔT` clamped — in release clamp to range, increment `graph_saturations[field]` and continue; in debug `GraphFault.raise` halts. Every test suite asserts `graph_saturations` is all zeros, so a rounding defect still fails CI without ejecting a player from a 40-minute session. Work per run is bounded by construction (D.16): one pass per loop, `MAX_SPREAD_PER_STEP` failures, `MAX_FOOTPRINTS_PER_STEP` writes, never catch-up; `STEP_BUDGET_MS` (registry 4 ms, "Diagnostic alarm for P3; target is 2 ms") is the kernel's logging threshold, not a cut-off, and the Graph's own budget of record is the **spike share** it asks of that 2 ms target — the sum of the Graph hooks that run on one hook tick at **P99 ≤ 0.5 ms and max ≤ 1.0 ms** on a release export (Godot 4.7.2 release template, `forward_plus`, vsync on, 120-frame warm-up) on RM-DESKTOP and RM-DECK (delegated ruling R12; `project.yaml` `qa.min_spec`; pending spike measurement; pass needs P99 within the R12 headroom of the frame ceiling) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — delegated ruling R12)* — with the allocation, read-side and decision-ladder clauses of D.16; the first draft's "10 ms at 10 Hz" framing was wrong, because the constraint is a spike on one 60 Hz frame in six that every other divisor-6 hook shares, and the technical-director's apportionment of the 2 ms target is Open Question 31 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.

**LS-25 — Endogenous rates: wear, compression, stress.** Everything that damages a subject without a player or a horror doing it is one of: overload (D.9) and the short burn of a FAILED cable in SHORT (D.9) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, wear on period steps (LEAK, OVERHEAT, fatigue, patch decay, and **duty** — `W_DUTY_BP_PER_100` on every running SCRUBBER, PUMP, REACTOR and HEATER, D.9, so that a fully repaired ship is quiet for a session but never permanently safe (P5): at 2 bp per period a healthy machine reaches `HP_OK` after 1500 periods = 4.2 h of continuous duty and carries WORN from then on *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)*), thaw-burst (C5). All of it is multiplied by the section's `rate_pm` (HOME 1000; DERELICT `10^6 / DERELICT_FAULT_COMPRESSION` = 2000, so a derelict fault's wear clock runs at half length; clocks set by a stock draining — a tank emptying, a pool venting — do not compress) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* and by `stress_pm` (`BREACH_PROPAGATION_MULT` 2000 while the section is stressed by M17 STRESS, 1000 otherwise). The multipliers never touch gas exchange, heat conduction or supply: the breach rhythm makes the ship *fail* faster, not *leak* faster — the hole still sets the hiss. On a derelict, faults that are "dead" (dried trails) are authored as subjects at `hp` ≤ `HP_FAIL` with a `def_id` whose module is powered-off, so they emit descriptors with `intensity_pm` 0 and `since_steps` large (the dried trail) and only wake when power or coolant reaches them.

**LS-26 — The MVP slice (R23) and what waits.** *Mode count note (consistency check 2026-10-26): "six MVP modes" are six hazard groups — LEAK, SHORT/OVERLOAD, OVERHEAT/SCRAM, FREEZE, BREACH, STARVE — spanning ids 1–7 and 9; SEVERED (8) is also MVP-live by construction because salvage (V8, M6/M12) produces an `open_end`; WORN (10) and SEAL_STRAIN (26) are non-hazard and live wherever hp or a closed hatch exists.* Home-ship (handmade): 5 compartments — cryo bay, corridor, engine room, cargo bay, airlock/pod dock — with all five loops: 1 REACTOR, 1 BATTERY, 2 CABLE_JUNCTIONs with breakers, ~8 LOADs (lamps 20 W at `waste_permille` 1000, door motors, scrubber 600 W at 400 ‰, pump 400 W at 300 ‰, one HEATER 500 W at 1000 ‰ in the cryo bay — the heat budget that keeps the ship at COMFORT, D.15 HEALTHY), ~8 CABLEs; 1 SCRUBBER, 1 O2_TANK (3,000,000 mGU), 5 VENTs, ~5 DUCTs (one exposed, in the cryo bay, authored as a spur so that its FAILED state cuts no vent's `path_cap`, D.15 O2-SHIP) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; 1 RESERVOIR (20,000 mL), 1 PUMP, 1 RADIATOR, 3 PIPEs; 1 FUEL_TANK, 2 FUEL PIPEs; 7 HULL_SEGMENTs (two breachable per outer room); 1 weld socket. ~40 nodes, ~55 edges (all counts in this rule are estimates; Derelict Authoring's templates are of record, e.g. 11 home-ship LOADs) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from derelict-authoring-handmade-kits)*. Derelict A (cold wreck, first sortie — the home-ship is the spawn; registry `mvp_template_roster`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from derelict-authoring-handmade-kits, boarding-and-salvage)*: 4 compartments, dead bus, 1 BATTERY at partial charge, scrubber FAILED (`hp` 2000) to salvage (first V8), one frozen and leaking PIPE, 4 segments; ~30 nodes. Derelict B (hot wreck, second sortie, the weld donor): 5 compartments, live REACTOR in SCRAM, one latent seam fault, a hull breach, coolant leak behind the overheat; ~35 nodes. ≤ 12 compartments at the weld (this bounds the welded body, home + B = 10, not the loaded set {home + B, A} = 14) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from derelict-authoring-handmade-kits, modular-ship-assembly)*. The eight faults, each on a D.15 fixture: O2 pinhole at a clamp (exposed DUCT LEAK — the ship clock), O2 seam leak after weld (`A_seam_m` — the room clock, D.15 SEAM-WEEP), bus overload (PWR-OVL), broken breaker (a CABLE_JUNCTION at `hp` 2000, branch dark until replaced, D.15 BROKEN-BREAKER), coolant leak (CLT-LEAK), pump stall (PUMP STARVE on a dead branch → C3, D.15 PUMP-STALL), fuel flange drip (FUEL-DRIP) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*, hull fatigue → breach (HULL-FATIGUE). Verbs: V1 patch, V3 breaker, V4 bulkhead, V5 valve, V6 emergency vent, V8 salvage, V9 weld, V12 hand crank = M11 `reset_node` only, LS-18 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*, **V7 top-up** — **an MVP verb by user ruling (D34, D40, D70b, X5, 2026-10-27)**: a deliberate, actively chosen O2 tap at a lit port, which reaches the Graph as a Suit-side M16 `withdraw_store` call at the tank port (the Graph's M16 was MVP-live already and gains no rule); Suit runs `TOPUP_MODE` VERB with `CELL_ENABLED` on, so the lamp cell and the Pillar 3 "go dark" lever exist at MVP; Open Question 20 is resolved *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; modes live: LEAK, SHORT/OVERLOAD, OVERHEAT/SCRAM, FREEZE, BREACH, STARVE (plus WORN on every duty-worn machine and SEAL_STRAIN at the closed seam hatch — both live in MVP by construction, neither a hazard mode; *Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN*). The first fault the player meets is a coolant frost trail from the cryo bay (a coolant PIPE LEAK in a cryo bay authored at `T` −2000 mC, FROST band, so the drip frosts — the art bible's carried element). MVP must prove: C2/C3/C6 readable from the world; one weld joining a derelict (power + air + opening) with physical consequences (weep at the seam, draught when the hatch opens); save v1 round-trip with `state_hash`; the six modes live. **Waits for VS-A:** fire and C9, C10 and STRUT load, HEAT_EXCHANGER, FRAME_NODE, FUEL_PORT, CO2, V2/V10/V11, procedural subgraph templates, multiple reservoirs, footprint kinds beyond the five, quiescence sleeping, compaction and Web Map deltas, the hook stagger, power cycles.

### States and Transitions

**Node and edge lifecycle.** `state` is evaluated in phase 9 from `hp`, `forced_mode` and the mutators that ran in P2. "Gate" means the D.11 gate for endogenous causes; exogenous causes are never gated.

| From | To | Guard | Who triggers | Descriptor emitted |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| OK | DEGRADED | `hp < HP_OK` | phase 8 damage (D.9), M17 GOUGE, M18 | `N/<id>`; subject descriptor at sev 1 — WORN (PATCH_WEEP on a patched subject) unless a mode 1–9 is already live (`warn_since_step` set) *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)* |
| DEGRADED | OK | `hp ≥ HP_OK` and `forced_mode` = 0 | M7 FULL repair (PATCH cannot: cap 6000) | `N/<id>`; descriptor cleared (`warn_since_step` 0) |
| DEGRADED | FAILED | `hp ≤ HP_FAIL` **and gate open**, or `forced_mode` set | phase 9 (endogenous, gated); M17 RUPTURE/GOUGE, M18 EXOGENOUS, M9 overload-by-breaker (ungated) | `N/<id>`; sev ≥ 3; `fail_since_step` set; STARVE descriptors on dependants next run |
| OK | FAILED | exogenous damage below `HP_FAIL` in one apply (RUPTURE, RAGGED cut stub) | M17, M3, M18 EXOGENOUS | as above, `cause` EXOGENOUS |
| OK / DEGRADED | FAILED (forced) | FREEZE: `T_room < FREEZE_ON_MC` with ≥ 100 steps of the FREEZE pre-stage *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)*; SCRAM: OVERHEAT sustained `OVERHEAT_SCRAM_STEPS` or `starve_steps ≥ FUEL_STARVE_STEPS`; SEVERED: cut/salvage left `open_end` | phase 9 (gated), M3 RAGGED, M6 | `N/<id>`; mode descriptor sev ≥ 3 |
| FAILED | DEGRADED | `hp > HP_FAIL` and `forced_mode` = 0 | M7 (PATCH or FULL); M11 `reset_node` clearing SCRAM/FREEZE when the clear condition holds; thaw at `T_room > FREEZE_OFF_MC` (which opens the thaw window, LS-9) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* | `N/<id>`; sev falls to the hp-derived value |
| FAILED | DESTROYED | `hp` = 0 **and gate open** (endogenous) or any exogenous damage to 0 | phase 8, M17, M18 | `N/<id>`; sev 4; BREACH at `A_HOLE_MAX` for a hull |
| OK / DEGRADED / FAILED / DESTROYED | SALVAGED | node is a module (not COMPARTMENT/EXTERIOR/HULL_SEGMENT) | M6 | `N/<id>` + `T`; connected edges' ends `open_end` → SEVERED descriptors |
| SALVAGED / ABSENT | OK | hardpoint ports match `def_id` | M5 `install_module` (`hp = hp_start`) | `N/<id>` + `T` |
| any | (tombstone, no state) | section discarded | M4 | `T` + `SEC/<id>` |
| DESTROYED | DEGRADED/OK by repair | — | **rejected** `NOT_REPAIRABLE` | none |

**Mode overlays** (independent of `state` unless they force FAILED): LEAK on/off at `LEAK_HP` (edge: `N/<id>`, `F/<id>` on intensity bucket); OVERLOAD on/off at `ρ` 1000; STARVE on/off at `served_pm` 1000 (status; `N/<id>`); OVERHEAT on after `OVERHEAT_ON_STEPS` above `OVERHEAT_ON_MC`, off below `OVERHEAT_OFF_MC`; FREEZE pre-stage on/off at `FREEZE_WARN_MC`; BREACH on at `hp < BREACH_HP` (gated), off at `hp ≥ BREACH_HP`; SEVERED on by cut/salvage, off by `cap_port`; WORN on while `hp < HP_OK` with no mode 1–9 live and no patch, off otherwise (`N/<id>` only on the state change; module-body channel, LS-14); PATCH_WEEP on while `hp_cap < HP_FULL`, `hp < HP_OK` and no mode 1–9 live (consequence channel, LS-11) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)*; SHORT on while `hp ≤ HP_FAIL` and energised, off at `hp > HP_FAIL` or when no end island holds a live source (`N/<id>`; consequence channel) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — confirming review, blocker 1)*.

**Compartment band states** (each of `band_o2`, `band_p`, `band_t` is its own state machine over the LS-7 table; a transition in the worsening direction fires at the boundary, in the improving direction at boundary + `H`; the band held at the end of a run is persisted (LS-21) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; every transition emits `B/<cid>` and the matching `ATMO_*` descriptor severity): NORMAL ⇄ THIN ⇄ HYPOXIC ⇄ ANOXIC; NORMAL ⇄ LOW ⇄ DECOMP ⇄ VACUUM; SCORCH ⇄ HOT ⇄ COMFORT ⇄ COOL ⇄ FROST ⇄ FREEZING ⇄ DEEP_COLD. Transitions are driven only by phases 4–7 and M15/M17 CHILL/BURN; a band never changes on a tick where no Graph hook ran. `pressure_trend` is derived, not a state.

**Seam states.** `ACTIVE` (created by M2 with every sub-edge disconnected; weeps per D.2; sub-edges opened by M8/M9/M13) → `ACTIVE` with `leak_bp` 0 after wear-in or a seam PATCH (M7; V11 in VS-A) → `CUT` (M3; SeamRecord kept as a tombstone with the boundary mode; sub-edges tombstoned; `S/<seam_id>` + `T`). A seam's sub-edges carry their own LS-10 lifecycle (a seam CABLE can overload and fail like any cable); the SeamRecord's `hp` is the hull-side seam integrity that D.2's BREACH reads.

**Block states.** `EMPTY` (`default_state()`: no sections, counters at 1, `last_step_no` 0) → `LIVE` (after M1 or a successful restore; hooks run; mutators accept) → `RESTORING` (two-phase restore into scratch; the live block is untouched until the swap; `GRAPH_RESTORE_*` on failure leaves it LIVE with the pre-restore state) → `FAULTED` (kernel state after a structural invariant failure in any build or a numeric `GraphFault` in debug: hooks and mutators stop, the kernel owns the exit — dev halt or release-to-menu with no autosave; the partial writes of the failing apply are left in place for the diagnostic dump). The Graph never transitions itself out of FAULTED; a new world or a restore of the last good snapshot does.

The Graph reads `waste_permille`, `R_SCR`, `BATT_CELL`, `cap_ml` and `out_w_rated` live through `def_id` — never copied at M5, never persisted. | Interaction | verbs V1–V12 → M7, M8, M9, M10, M11, M12, M13, M6, M16 | `node()`/`edge()` for lit-object state; `BLOCKED` results | Graph publishes; Interaction owns the commands and channel timing | P2 apply; `validate` via `check_*` (names published under LS-18; V6 → M13) | designed (`interaction-system.md`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from interaction-system)* |
| Save / Load | `restore(data, stored_version)`, snapshot calls | `canonical_bytes()`, `GRAPH_RESTORE_*` reasons; S-1 constraint; `soonest_warning_remaining(loaded_only)` for the autosave tell | Save/Load; S-1 routed | P6 snapshot (boundary services) at any tick boundary — the pending input queues are part of the block (LS-21) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* | designed *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from save-load-and-versioned-format)* |
| Web Map | M19 `set_section_dormant` at load/unload; M16 hop-fuel withdrawals from the home FUEL_TANK (mL); M4 in VS-A | section list, `section(section_id)`, `discarded_*`, `withdrawn_*` | Graph in MVP; VS-A deltas Web Map's (ADR-LSG-04); the 'pod reserve' band on the home FUEL_TANK is static prop art carrying no state, not a consequence-channel item | P2 apply | designed (`web-map-and-transit.md`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from web-map-and-transit)* |
| Boarding & Salvage | none directly | M6 / M12 and the consequence channel reach Boarding through Interaction; the hum question (C-BS-4) is open | Graph | — | designed *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from boarding-and-salvage)* |
| HUD & Suit Layer | none | reads no Graph state; cites the O2 band edges (LS-7) and `ALARM_SEV_MIN` | Graph owns the constants | presentation (soft, downstream) | designed *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from hud-and-suit-layer)* |
| Debug Overlay (True Values) | none (never writes) | `true_value(kind, id)`, `block_hash()`, `summary()`, `failures(filter)`, `compartment(cid)`, `power_use(section_id)` and the `graph_*` counters incl. `graph_saturations[field]`, as DIAGNOSTIC | Graph | presentation (soft) | designed *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from debug-overlay-true-values)* |
| Resource Depletion & Wear (VS) | wear rate overrides per `def_id` (would replace the LS-25 constants) | `hp`, `patched_at_step`, `hp_cap` | Graph owns `hp`; Wear proposes rates | — | VS, not designed |
| Failure Contract & Re-wake (VS) | none in MVP (death = restart from autosave, R16, user's call) | the warned cause (`warn_elapsed` on the lethal descriptor) | — | — | VS |
| Co-op replication (VS-B) | none (host-authoritative) | `summary()` SHARED_SUMMARY, deltas by dirty keys | Graph | P5 | VS-B |
| Onboarding | none | the first fault (coolant frost trail, cryo bay) and its descriptor sequence | Graph provides the fixture; Onboarding scripts nothing in the sim | — | provisional |

## Formulas

*Scope note.* Every formula below is integer arithmetic over the fields of `graph.state` and the immutable tuning table `GraphTuning`; nothing here reads Attention, sanity, rhythm state or any `PERCEPTION_OUT` value (LS-21 — a formula whose inputs include any of those is a Pillar 4 violation, not a tuning choice). **Every numeric default is provisional until spikes S0–S3 and the MVP playtest** (Section H); the time-band figures in D.15 are the acceptance targets the defaults were tuned to, not the other way round. Units are the R1 game units: gas in mGU (`GAS_SCALE` 1000 mGU per GU; 1 GU is the amount that gives 0.1 kPa in a 1-tile volume), volume in tiles, pressure derived in dkPa (standard `STD_PRESSURE_DKPA` 1000, standard O2 partial pressure `STD_O2_PP_DKPA` 210), temperature in mC, power in W which is identically dJ per step, energy in dJ, liquids in mL, integrity in basis points 0..10000, fractions in permille, and — on the gas-flow path only — opening area in milli flow units (`A_m` = 1000 × flow units) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. One step is `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` = 6 ticks = 0.1 s at `TICK_RATE` 60; "per step" is the only rate anywhere in this section. Source column vocabulary follows the kernel GDD: `constant` = compiled, changing it is a code change; `data file` = read at boot from `assets/data/life_support_graph.json` into `GraphTuning` (a balance pass edits the file, not the code; a designer-authored float is converted once at boot by `int(round(x × scale))` and never hashed); `calculated` = derived at runtime and never persisted; `state` = a persisted field of `graph.state` (hashed, replicated by summary); `registry` = a locked cross-system fact cited verbatim from `design/registry/entities.yaml`. Three arithmetic rules hold everywhere and are not repeated per formula: (1) **every flux is a non-negative magnitude with an explicit direction**, so integer division is applied only to non-negative operands and truncation is `floor` — written `⌊x⌋`; where a ceiling is wanted it is written `⌈x⌉` and computed as `(num + den − 1) / den` on non-negative operands; the only signed divisions are the two remainder-carrying thermal steps (D.7, D.8), which truncate toward zero and carry the remainder so nothing is lost; (2) **no `sqrt`, `exp`, `pow` or `sin` on the propagation path** — D.15 uses `ln` because it is a design-time estimate evaluated by the test harness, never by the sim; (3) every product the propagation code forms appears in D.13's table with its operand maxima, and the boot assertion there refuses to start a build whose table violates `max_a × max_b < 2^62`.

**D.1 Opening gas flow — mGU moved through one opening per step**

The `opening_gas_flow` formula is defined as:

`g = n_a × V_b − n_b × V_a` (signed, exact; gas moves from `a` to `b` when `g > 0`, from `b` to `a` when `g < 0`, not at all when `g = 0`) ; `Δn = min( ⌊G_OPEN × A_m × abs(g) / (V_a × V_b × GAS_SCALE × 1000)⌋ , ⌊abs(g) / ((V_a + V_b) × deg_max)⌋ )` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

Derived: `abs(g) / (V_a × V_b × GAS_SCALE)` is the exact pressure difference in dkPa, so the first term is `G_OPEN × A × ΔP` with `A = A_m / 1000` and the second is the old equalising amount over the degree; the floored `P_a`, `P_b` of D.3 serve bands, display and dirty-key quanta only and **never enter a flux**, so a floor can neither reverse the flux near balance nor break the monotone-gradient property of D.12 (the first draft took `ΔP` from the floored values and could)
Derived: when the lower side is EXTERIOR (`P_EXT` = 0), the first term is `⌊G_OPEN × A_m × n_tot_src / (V_src × GAS_SCALE × 1000)⌋`, the second is `⌊n_tot_src / deg_src⌋` and `Δn` is added to the source section's `vented_mgu`
Derived: species split `Δn_o2 = ⌊Δn × n_o2_src / n_tot_src⌋` (0 when `n_tot_src` = 0), `Δn_inert = Δn − Δn_o2`
Derived: per-step fraction `k = G_OPEN × A / (V_src × GAS_SCALE)` — the single number every time-to-band estimate in D.15 is built from

**Variables:**
| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|----------|------|-------|--------|-------------|
| G_OPEN | int | 1–10 (default **2**) | data file | mGU moved per (area unit × dkPa × step); the decompression-speed knob |
| A_m | int | 0–512,000 (door 64,000; pinhole 1,000; a seam weep may be any value ≥ 1) | state (`area` × 1000 of a stored OPENING) or calculated (D.2: `A_hole` × 1000, or the seam weep `A_seam_m`) | Opening area in milli flow units (1000 = one flow unit = 1/64 of a door aperture, LS-5) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| P_a, P_b | int | 0–1500 dkPa | calculated (D.3, from the start-of-phase snapshot) | Floored display pressures on each side (bands, `AIRFLOW` intensity = `min(1000, |P_a − P_b|)`, D.12); EXTERIOR reads `P_EXT` = 0 (R14); **not an input of `Δn`** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| V_a, V_b | int | 4–200 tiles | state (cached from Layout, LS-6) | Volumes |
| GAS_SCALE | int | 1000 | constant (registry candidate) | mGU per GU |
| deg_max | int | 1–16 | calculated (topology refresh) | `max(deg_a, deg_b)` where `deg` counts the OPENING edges and virtual holes of a compartment with area > 0; EXTERIOR counts as degree 1 — the D.12 stability divisor |
| n_a, n_b, n_o2_src, n_tot_src | int | 0–`n_max(c)` = `P_MAX` × `V` × `GAS_SCALE` (≤ 3×10^8 mGU) | state | Gas amounts and composition of the two compartments at the start of the phase *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| g | int | −6×10^10..+6×10^10 | calculated | Exact signed gradient `n_a × V_b − n_b × V_a` *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| Δn | int | 0–`n_tot_src` | calculated | mGU moved this step |

**Output Range:** 0 to the equalisation amount divided by the degree; it never overshoots (the second term is the amount that would exactly equalise two finite volumes, or everything the room holds against vacuum) and it never goes negative (magnitude with direction); the sign of the flux is always the sign of the true gradient `g`, even when both rooms read the same floored dkPa *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. It is 0 when `A_m` = 0, when `g` = 0, and — the truncation bias TD required this document to state — whenever `G_OPEN × A × ΔP < 1`: **equalisation stalls within `⌈1/k⌉` mGU** of the exact balance, which for a pinhole (`A` = 1, `V` = 20) is 10,000 mGU = 0.5 dkPa in that room and for a door is 156 mGU. This is expected behaviour, not a defect: the residual is below the 10 dkPa dirty-key quantum and below anything a gauge or a lung can register. Maximum for a door at full vacuum: `2 × 64 × 1000` = 128,000 mGU/step (a 20-tile room empties through an open hatch in about 160 steps = 16 s; LOW band in 5.6 s — the "chaos" end of the scale, Section C LS-7). The model is linear and unchoked: a vent through a hole slows as the pressure falls, which is the readable behaviour (the hiss fades as the room dies) and the reason no table or square-root law is needed.
**Example:** room a (`V_a` = 20, `P_a` = 1000, `n_tot` = 20,000,000, `n_o2` = 4,200,000, `deg_a` = 2) to room b (`V_b` = 40, `P_b` = 400, `deg_b` = 3) through a door `A` = 64: `g` = 20,000,000 × 40 − 16,000,000 × 20 = 480,000,000 (exact `ΔP` 600); first term `2 × 64,000 × 480,000,000 / (20 × 40 × 1000 × 1000)` = 76,800; second term `480,000,000 / (60 × 3)` = 2,666,666; `Δn` = 76,800; `Δn_o2` = `⌊76,800 × 4,200,000 / 20,000,000⌋` = 16,128; `Δn_inert` = 60,672. After the step `n_a` = 19,923,200 → `P_a` = `⌊19,923,200 / 20,000⌋` = 996; `n_b` = 16,000,000 + 76,800 → `P_b` = `⌊16,076,800 / 40,000⌋` = 401. Pinhole to vacuum from the same room: `Δn` = `min(⌊2 × 1,000 × 20,000,000 / (20 × 1000 × 1000)⌋, ⌊20,000,000 / 2⌋)` = 2,000 mGU/step, `k` = 0.0001 per step.

**D.2 Leak geometry and rates from integrity — virtual hole, seam weep, duct and pipe leaks**

The `leak_from_integrity` formula is defined as:

`A_hole = ⌈A_HOLE_MAX × (BREACH_HP − hp) / BREACH_HP⌉` if `hp < BREACH_HP`, else 0

Derived: `A_seam_m = L_seam × ⌊A_SEAM_MAX × leak_bp × (1000 − q)² / 10^7⌋` in milli flow units (1000 = one flow unit) — a **floor**, not the first draft's ceiling, so the weep falls with `leak_bp` from the first period *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
Derived: `leak_mgu = min( pool , ⌈LEAK_MGU_MAX × (LEAK_HP − hp) / LEAK_HP⌉ )` for a DUCT edge or O2_TANK node in mode LEAK; the gas goes to the subject's compartment (`exposed` = false) or to EXTERIOR and `vented_mgu` (`exposed` = true)
Derived: `leak_ml = min( stock , ⌈LEAK_ML_MAX × (LEAK_HP − hp) / LEAK_HP⌉ )` for a PIPE edge, RESERVOIR or FUEL_TANK in mode LEAK; coolant goes to the section's `spilled_cool_ml`, fuel to the compartment's `fuel_puddle_ml`; a PIPE leak draws from the loop's store — the RESERVOIR of its coolant component, the FUEL_TANK of its fuel component, debited in ascending node id, and from nothing when that store is empty *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

**Variables:**
| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|----------|------|-------|--------|-------------|
| hp | int | 0–10000 bp | state | Integrity of the hull segment, seam, duct, pipe or tank |
| BREACH_HP | int | 1000–6000 (default 4000) | data file | Below this a hull segment is BREACHED and feeds D.1 as a virtual opening to EXTERIOR |
| A_HOLE_MAX | int | 32–128 (default 64) | data file | Hole area at `hp` = 0 — one door's worth |
| A_hole | int | 0–A_HOLE_MAX | calculated | Virtual opening area; not stored, re-derived each run from `hp` |
| q | int | 400–990 ‰ (registry: `weld_quality`) | state (SeamRecord, set by Assembly at weld, D.14) | Weld quality |
| leak_bp | int | 0–10000 | state (SeamRecord; starts at `SEAM_LEAK_INITIAL_BP`, D.14) | How much of the seam's geometric imperfection currently weeps; wear-in and SEAL_SEAM (V11, VS-A) lower it |
| A_SEAM_MAX | int | 4–32 (default 16) | data file | Area per seam tile at `q` = 0 and `leak_bp` = 10000 |
| L_seam | int | 1–12 tiles | state (SeamRecord) | Seam length |
| A_seam_m | int | 0–384,000 (milli flow units) | calculated | Seam weep area to EXTERIOR for the **whole seam**; it is divided between the seam's compartments (each gets `⌊A_seam_m / n_comp⌋`, the remainder to the lowest compartment id) and a compartment's `k` is built from its own share, never from the seam total *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| LEAK_HP | int | 2000–6000 (default 4000) | data file | Below this a duct, pipe or tank is in mode LEAK |
| LEAK_MGU_MAX | int | 100–2000 (default **500**) | data file | Duct/tank gas leak at `hp` = 0 |
| LEAK_ML_MAX | int | 2–60 (default **20**) | data file | Pipe/reservoir/tank liquid leak at `hp` = 0 |
| pool, stock | int | ≥ 0 | state | What the leak can draw from this step (the pool of connected O2 stores; for a PIPE the component's RESERVOIR or FUEL_TANK stock; for a tank or reservoir its own liquid) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| leak_mgu, leak_ml | int | 0–max | calculated | Amount lost this step |

**Output Range:** `A_hole` is 0 at `hp` ≥ 4000, 1 at `hp` = 3999, 64 at `hp` = 0 (a DESTROYED segment is a door-sized hole). `A_seam_m` falls **linearly with `leak_bp`**: for `q` 850 it is 360 per tile at `leak_bp` 10000, 180 at 5000, 3 at 100 and 0 below `leak_bp` 28 (`q` never reaches 1000: `Q_MAX` 990, registry `weld_quality`), so wear-in reduces the weep from the first period and ends it a little before period 100. The first draft's ceiling held a good weld at one full flow unit per tile until `leak_bp` reached 0, ignored the split between the two rooms, and so drained a default weld's bay by 63–86 % before it tightened (its "20 min to LOW" was really about 2) — milli-units are the fix, because the weep of a good weld is a fraction of one flow unit *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. The weep never becomes silent while it flows: its descriptor keeps `intensity_pm` ≥ 1 (LS-14). `leak_mgu` and `leak_ml` are likewise at least 1 the moment `hp` drops below `LEAK_HP` (the first drip is the tell that opens the fairness window, D.11) and are bounded by what exists, so stocks never go negative. Nothing here is persisted: a save stores `hp`, `q`, `leak_bp` and `L_seam` and the geometry is re-derived.
**Example:** hull segment `hp` = 1500 → `A_hole` = `⌈64 × 2500 / 4000⌉` = `⌈40.0⌉` = 40. Fatigued segment at `hp` = 3950 → `⌈64 × 50 / 4000⌉` = `⌈0.8⌉` = 1 (a pinhole). Seam `q` = 850, `L_seam` = 3, `leak_bp` = 10000: `⌊16 × 10000 × 150² / 10^7⌋` = 360 per tile → `A_seam_m` = 1,080, shared by the two compartments = 540 each. A 24-tile cargo bay then has `k` = `2 × 0.54 / 24,000` = 4.5 × 10^−5 per step (1,080 mGU/step at 1000 dkPa), tapering linearly to 0 over 10,000 steps: with no vent help the bay loses 20 % of its gas, ends at 797 dkPa, still NORMAL for total pressure (its O2 band reads THIN from about step 4,325 if the vent is isolated, D.15 SEAM-WEEP) — findable, fixable, not an emergency. (The first draft put the whole seam in one 20-tile room, `k` = 2 × 3 / 20,000 and "20 min to LOW"; with the split and the taper the bay never reaches LOW.) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Duct at `hp` = 2000: `leak_mgu` = `⌈500 × 2000 / 4000⌉` = 250 mGU/step. Coolant pipe at `hp` = 3800: `leak_ml` = `⌈20 × 200 / 4000⌉` = `⌈1.0⌉` = 1 mL/step; at `hp` = 3000: 5 mL/step.

**D.3 Atmosphere derivation and bands — pressure, O2 partial pressure, fraction, band with hysteresis**

The `atmosphere_derive` formula is defined as:

`P = ⌊(n_o2 + n_inert) / (V × GAS_SCALE)⌋` ; `p_o2 = ⌊n_o2 / (V × GAS_SCALE)⌋` ; `o2_pm = ⌊1000 × n_o2 / (n_o2 + n_inert)⌋` (0 when the room is empty)

Derived: `band = lookup(value, table LS-7)` with hysteresis — a band is left the step its boundary is crossed in the worsening direction and re-entered only once the value is `H` beyond the boundary in the improving direction (`H_P` = 5 dkPa for `P` and `p_o2`, `H_T` = 500 mC for `T`); the band held at the end of the previous run is the hysteresis memory and is persisted (`band_o2`, `band_p`, `band_t`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
Derived: `pressure_trend = sign(P − P_prev_run)` ∈ {−1, 0, +1}, from the previous run's derived `P` (`p_prev`, persisted — LS-21 — so a restored run reads the same trend as an uninterrupted one) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

**Variables:**
| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|----------|------|-------|--------|-------------|
| n_o2, n_inert | int | 0–`n_max(c)` = `P_MAX` × `V` × `GAS_SCALE` (`P_MAX` 1500 dkPa; ≤ 3×10^8 mGU at `V` 200; 4.2×10^7 at standard pressure in the 42-tile cryo bay) | state | Gas amounts per compartment; a value above `n_max(c)` is clamped in release (LS-24) and refused on restore *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| V | int | 4–200 tiles | state (cached from Layout) | Volume |
| P, p_o2 | int | 0–1500 dkPa | calculated | Total and O2 partial pressure |
| o2_pm | int | 0–1000 ‰ | calculated | O2 fraction (display only; physiology and bands use `p_o2`) |
| H_P, H_T | int | 0–20 dkPa (default 5) / 0–2000 mC (default 500) | data file | Hysteresis margins |
| band_o2, band_p, band_t | enum | LS-7 tables | calculated | The felt consequence; the single source of truth for Suit, Failure & Trail, Audio |

**Output Range:** all non-negative; `p_o2 ≤ P` always; `P` for a standard room is 1000 and `p_o2` 210. Pressure depends on `n` and `V` only — there is no temperature-to-pressure coupling (R14), so a cold room does not read low and a hot one does not read high; what the gauge says is what the lungs get. Hysteresis means a value oscillating within 5 dkPa of a boundary flips the band once, not every run; the band change is what dirties `B/<cid>` (D.10).
**Example:** `V` = 20, `n_o2` = 2,900,000, `n_inert` = 15,800,000 → `P` = `⌊18,700,000 / 20,000⌋` = 935 (NORMAL, ≥ 700); `p_o2` = `⌊2,900,000 / 20,000⌋` = 145 (THIN, 140..179); `o2_pm` = `⌊1000 × 2,900,000 / 18,700,000⌋` = 155. If `p_o2` then rises to 182 the band stays THIN until it reaches 185 (`180 + H_P`).

**D.4 Module efficiency and severity from integrity**

The `efficiency_and_severity` formula is defined as:

`eff = clamp( (hp − HP_FAIL) × 1000 / (HP_OK − HP_FAIL) , 0 , 1000 )` ; `sev = 0` if `hp ≥ HP_OK`, else `min( 4 , 1 + (HP_OK − hp) / SEV_STEP )`

Derived: a non-zero `forced_mode` (FREEZE, SCRAM, SEVERED) sets `eff` = 0 and `sev = max(sev, 3)`; a hazard mode may raise `sev` above the hp-derived value by its own rule (a BREACH with `A_hole` ≥ 32 is sev 4; an OVERLOAD at `ρ` ≥ 1500 is sev 3)

**Variables:**
| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|----------|------|-------|--------|-------------|
| hp | int | 0–10000 bp | state | Node or edge integrity |
| HP_OK | int | 5000–9000 (default 7000) | data file (registry candidate `HP_OK`) | At or above: OK; below: DEGRADED |
| HP_FAIL | int | 1000–4000 (default 3000) | data file (registry candidate `HP_FAIL`) | At or below: FAILED |
| HP_FULL | int | 10000 | constant (registry candidate `HP_FULL`) | Full integrity; the `hp_cap` of an un-patched subject |
| SEV_STEP | int | 500–3000 (default 1500) | data file | bp per severity level |
| eff | int | 0–1000 ‰ | calculated | Output multiplier applied by D.5, D.6, D.8 |
| sev | int | 0–4 | calculated | Descriptor severity (LS-13); 1 is the trace that opens the D.11 window, 3 is the first `alarm_eligible` level (R7) |

**Output Range:** `eff` is 1000 at `hp` ≥ 7000, 0 at `hp` ≤ 3000 and linear between (a subject half-way through DEGRADED works at half output — the gauge reads it and the hum drops). `sev` is 0 above 7000, 1 at 5501..6999, 2 at 4001..5500, 3 at 2501..4000, 4 at ≤ 2500. The two thresholds are the state boundaries of LS-10 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*, so a state change and a severity change always coincide with a visible change in output.
**Example:** `hp` = 5000 → `eff` = `2000 × 1000 / 4000` = 500; `sev` = `1 + 2000 / 1500` = `1 + 1` = 2. A patched subject at its cap `hp` = 6000 → `eff` = 750, `sev` = 1: a patch is never silent (D.9 patch decay relies on this).

**D.5 O2 supply chain — scrubber output, vent injection, respiration**

The `o2_supply` formula is defined as:

`out_scr = min( ⌊R_SCR × eff × sat / 10^6⌋ , free )` with `free = Σ (stock_cap − stock)` over the component's O2_TANKs ; `mk_offer = Σ ⌊R_MAKEUP × eff × sat / 10^6⌋` over the component's scrubbers *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

Derived (per vent, ascending node id, the pool debited vent by vent — **sequential-greedy**, `pool` below being the running balance after the lower-id vents, and the one explicit exception to LS-1 (j)'s proportional scaling *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; O2 first, so life outranks pressure): `def_o2 = max(0, SP × V × GAS_SCALE − n_o2)` ; `def_in = max(0, SP_IN × V × GAS_SCALE − n_inert)` ; `bud = ⌊R_VENT × eff_v / 1000⌋` ; `inj_o2 = min( bud , pool , path_cap , def_o2 )` ; `inj_in = min( bud − inj_o2 , path_cap − inj_o2 , mk_left , def_in )`, where `mk_left` starts at `mk_offer` and every vent's `inj_in` debits it — the vent has **one budget**, spent on the O2 deficit first and on the inert deficit with what is left, and neither species is injected past its own setpoint *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
Derived: respiration `draw = min(request, n_o2)` per `draw_air` call (M15), applied in phase 0 of the next run in ascending compartment id; the shortfall `request − draw` is returned to Suit and is Suit's business, never the Graph's
Derived: pool accounting per O2 component per run: `pool' = pool + Σ out_scr − Σ inj_o2 − Σ leak_mgu`, with stores debited in ascending node id until the amount is met; `produced_mgu += Σ out_scr + Σ inj_in` and `breathed_mgu += Σ draw` — make-up is demand-driven (make-up no vent takes is never produced) and scrubber output beyond `free` is not produced, so the pool has a capacity and an overflow rule *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*

**Variables:**
| Variable | Type | Range | Source | Description |
|----------|------|-------|--------|-------------|
| R_SCR | int | 100–1000 (default 400) mGU/step | data file (module def, Item DB) | Nominal scrubber output |
| eff, eff_v | int | 0–1000 | calculated (D.4) | Scrubber and vent integrity multipliers |
| sat | int | 0–1000 | calculated (D.6, tier 0) | Power satisfaction of the scrubber's tier |
| R_VENT | int | 200–1500 (default 600) mGU/step | data file | Vent maximum injection — one budget shared by both species (the Item DB's vent port `cap` equals it) |
| R_MAKEUP | int | 600–6000 (default **1800**) mGU/step | data file | Scrubber inert make-up offered per component at full `eff` and power; demand-driven; ★ registry candidate *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| SP_IN | int | 700–900 dkPa (default **790** = `STD_PRESSURE_DKPA − STD_O2_PP_DKPA`) | data file (derived default) | Inert partial-pressure setpoint of every vent; not player-adjustable (M10 sets only `SP`) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| stock_cap | int | 1×10^6..10^7 mGU (default 3,000,000) | state (Item DB def, read live) | O2_TANK capacity; the scrubber is throttled to the free space *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
| pool | int | ≥ 0 | state (sum of connected O2_TANK stocks) | O2 available to this vent's component |
| path_cap | int | 0–duct cap | calculated (topology refresh) | Bottleneck DUCT or seam capacity between pool and vent; 0 if any VALVE on the only path is closed |
| SP | int | 140–230 dkPa (default 210) | state (vent setpoint, M10) | Target O2 partial pressure |
| p_o2 | int | 0–300 | calculated (D.3) | Current O2 partial pressure of the vent's compartment |
| R_BREATH | int | 100–600 (default 300) mGU/step per crew | data file (Suit-owned; the Graph only applies the request) | Respiration |
| out_scr, inj_o2, inj_in | int | 0–R_SCR, 0–R_VENT | calculated | mGU of O2 produced, O2 injected and inert injected this step |
| N_crew | int | 1–4 (`MAX_CREW`) | state (Suit, per room) | Crew breathing in the room; the room's respiration is `R_B` = `R_BREATH` × `N_crew` (D.15) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* |
