### Serialization & restore

- **GIVEN** F-MVP at T 1,800 after S2's weld, mid-breach, **WHEN** `snapshot()` runs, **THEN** it completes in ≤ 0.5 ms (F-K ≤ 3 ms), the snapshot is ≤ 32 KB, `restore()` + `post_restore()` complete in ≤ 2 ms (F-K ≤ 10 ms), and the canonical bytes immediately after restore are byte-identical to the snapshot's, tombstones included. (spike) [LS-21; S3 cost; ADR-LSG-05]
- **GIVEN** a corpus of 1,000 mutated snapshots generated from seeds 1..1000 of F-MVP's snapshot, each **pre-classified by construction** — class V (a valid edit that must restore: a changed `hp`, an `n_o2` within `n_max(c)`), class I (an invalid edit with one expected named reason: a truncated field → `BAD_LENGTH`; a swapped or duplicate tag → `BAD_TAG`; an endpoint out of range → `BAD_EDGE_ENDPOINT`; an enum out of range → `BAD_ENUM`; `stored_version` 0 and current + 1 → `BAD_VERSION`; a value 1 above its max, including `n_o2 + n_inert` 1 above `n_max(c)` → `OUT_OF_RANGE`; a queue over its bound → `QUEUE`; ids out of order → `ID_ORDER`; a counter below the max id → `COUNTER`), and class B (a single bit flip in a numeric payload, whose outcome must be either a restore or exactly one reason from the closed set and nothing else) — **WHEN** each is restored, **THEN** every V restores and hashes to its expected value, every I fails with exactly its expected reason, every B ends in one of the two allowed outcomes, the live block is byte-identical before and after every failure, and zero script errors escape; the corpus is regenerated by seed, never edited by hand, and the test prints the class counts. (auto, slow) [LS-21; Edge Cases "restore of a corrupt snapshot"; S3 corruption] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **GIVEN** a restore with Layout not yet restored, **WHEN** `post_restore` runs, **THEN** it fails with `GRAPH_RESTORE_UNKNOWN_ROOM`; **GIVEN** a save whose `def_id` is unknown to the Item DB, **THEN** `GRAPH_RESTORE_UNKNOWN_DEF(def_id)`. (auto) [LS-21; LS-6]
- **GIVEN** a synthetic v1 → v2 migration adding one field with a default, **WHEN** the retained v1 golden save in `tests/` is restored by a v2 build, **THEN** it round-trips and its post-migration hash equals the golden v2 hash. (auto) [LS-21; S3 migration]
- **GIVEN** the persisted-field list of LS-21, **WHEN** `canonical_bytes()` is diffed against the fields the next run reads, **THEN** every field the run depends on is in the snapshot (`t_rem`, `tc_rem`, `fuel_rem`, `out_w`, `forced_mode_next`, `warn_since_step` and `has_warn`, `thaw_until_step`, `mode_since_step`, `mode_key`, `isolated_at_step`, `dormant`, `dormant_since_step`, the section counters, `def_table`, the per-hook step cursor, the three `pending_*` queues, the band, `p_prev` and SEAL_STRAIN memory, the compartment warn trackers `warn_since_o2/p/t` and `warn_bits` *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*) and no derived cache is. **Method A (reflection):** the test enumerates every member of `GraphState` by reflection and fails if one is neither written by `canonical_bytes()` nor on the checked-in DERIVED allowlist (the non-persisted caches of LS-21). **Method B (zero and diff):** for each DERIVED-allowlist field the test zeroes it in a copy of a mid-fault state, runs one step, and asserts the copy's `state_hash` equals the unmodified control's — a step that reads a cache it did not first recompute shows as a differing hash, which is the mechanism for "a field the next run reads is missing from the snapshot". Both run; either failing fails the criterion. (auto) [LS-21] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings; re-review, disagreement settled per G2)*
- **GIVEN** a LEAK pipe on F-MVP whose `sev` rises 2 → 3 at step N and whose upstream valve is closed at step N + 80 and reopened at step N + 380 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings; GA-T, user ruling D30)*, **WHEN** the descriptor is read on steps N − 1, N, N + 79, N + 80, N + 200, N + 379, N + 380 and N + 381, **THEN** `mode_steps` reads (the previous mode's age), 0, 79, 80, 200, 379, 380, 381 and `grow_steps` reads (the previous mode's age), 0, 79, 80, 80, 80, 380, 381 exactly; `grow_steps` is never negative; `warn_since_step` is unchanged by the restamp at step N and by the isolation, so `since_steps` and `warn_elapsed` keep counting from it (the fairness gate reads `warn_since_step` only). - **GIVEN** a snapshot taken at any tick while a descriptor is isolated, **WHEN** it is restored and run to the same step as an uninterrupted copy, **THEN** `mode_since_step`, `mode_key`, `isolated_at_step` and `state_hash` equal the uninterrupted copy on every run, and no restamp occurs on the first post-restore run. A snapshot of a subject at `sev` 0 restores `mode_key` 0 and `mode_since_step` 0. (auto) [LS-14 GA-T; LS-21] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*

### Falsifiability

- **GIVEN** a degraded fixture and a Perception block set to "all OK", **WHEN** both worlds run to tick N, **THEN** `state_hash` of `graph.state` is identical with and without the Perception content. (auto) [LS-22; CR-12]
- **GIVEN** every mutator signature, **WHEN** reviewed, **THEN** no parameter is a believed, perceived or displayed state; all are ids, magnitudes, enums or plans. (review) [LS-22]
- **GIVEN** the LS-22 witness table, **WHEN** each gauge quantity's true value is changed in a fixture, **THEN** the named witness descriptor or band changes on the same run (`ATMO_THIN` with `p_o2`, `ATMO_COLD` with `T`, `AIRFLOW` with `ΔP` across an open opening (ΔP 100 → `intensity_pm` 100, ΔP 200 → 200: the value moves on the same run and is never 0) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — confirming review, blocker 2)*, `SEAL_STRAIN` with `ΔP` across a closed one, `served_pm` with power, pipe LEAK/drip with coolant `hp`, hull BREACH with hull `hp`, `PUDDLE` with `fuel_puddle_ml`), and every witness named is on the consequence channel (`medium ≠ NONE`). (auto) [LS-22; LS-14 two channels] *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)*
- **GIVEN** the Failure & Trail, Instruments, Adaptive Audio and Perception Filter sources when written, **WHEN** scanned, **THEN** none suppresses a consequence-channel descriptor (`medium ≠ NONE`) whose `sev ≥ 1` from its acoustic or physical channel, none plays an alarm class without `alarm_eligible`, and the Filter is the only non-diagnostic caller of `true_value()`. (lint) [LS-13; LS-14 two channels; R17] *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)* Not suppression *(Amended 2026-10-27 — consistency pass, routed from adaptive-audio-and-tells, failure-and-trail-system)*: a flow-fraction descriptor (LEAK, hole-area BREACH flow, AIRFLOW, SEVERED, SEAL_STRAIN GAS/CREAK) at `intensity_pm` 0 is silent by the closed-valve rule (an AIRFLOW descriptor has `intensity_pm` ≥ 1 by construction, |ΔP| ≥ 1, so the closed-valve silence rule never applies to it) *(Amended 2026-10-28 — confirming review, blocker 2)*, and an empty STILL gas plume at flow zero (FT-18) is not suppression; the SEAL_STRAIN HEAT/TICK branch, whose `intensity_pm` measures `|ΔP|` and not its thermal cause, and every mode with no Graph-defined `intensity_pm` (LS-14 provenance) are driven by `sev` and never silenced. The Attention `attn` breach event is Attention's own tell, not a Graph descriptor, and is exempt from the alarm-gate wording.
- **GIVEN** the descriptor list of a fixture with one duty-worn SCRUBBER at `hp` 6998 and one of each mode 1–9 and 20–26 live, **WHEN** read through `failures()`, **THEN** the SCRUBBER's descriptor has `mode` WORN, `medium` NONE, `acoustic` NONE and `alarm_eligible` false, and every other descriptor has `medium ≠ NONE` and `acoustic ≠ NONE`; **GIVEN** a DUCT at `hp` 6998 (WORN) driven below `LEAK_HP` by M18, **THEN** its WORN descriptor is gone and a LEAK descriptor with `medium` GAS and `acoustic` HISS is in its place on the same run. (auto) [LS-11 WORN; LS-14 two channels; P4] *(Amended 2026-10-26 — CD-GDD-ALIGN)*; see also the FAILED-scrubber criterion in the failure-lifecycle group for the gate-open case *(Amended 2026-10-28 — cross-GDD fix)*
- **GIVEN** a PIPE patched to its cap (`hp` 6000, `hp_cap` 6000) and a SCRUBBER duty-worn to 6998, **WHEN** read through `failures()` with a Perception block set to "all OK" and again without it, **THEN** the pipe carries `mode` 27, `medium` COOLANT, `acoustic` DRIP, `sev` 1, `intensity_pm` 0 and `alarm_eligible` false, identically in both worlds, while the SCRUBBER carries WORN; the pipe's `warn_since_step` precedes its later re-leak by at least 2,400 steps, so the gated re-leak finds its gate open. (auto) [LS-11 PATCH_WEEP; LS-23; P1, P4] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **GIVEN** a hallucinated "fixed" pipe at true `hp` 2000, **WHEN** the player runs the repair channel, **THEN** `repair_step` increments are applied against 2000 and the room's cold and hiss descriptors persist until the true `hp` crosses `LEAK_HP`. (auto) [LS-22 guarantee 2]

(spike) [D.13; LS-1 (d)]

- **GIVEN** S0-e, **WHEN** `JSON.parse_string` / `JSON.to_native` are run on 2^53 + 1 and on a `PackedInt64Array`, **THEN** the behaviour is recorded and S-1's wording finalised. (spike) [LS-21; R6]
- **GIVEN** S0-f, **WHEN** each of the twelve phases of LS-8 runs alone over F-MVP and over a 4× synthetic fixture (10^3 iterations each, Release template), **THEN** a per-phase µs table replaces the three coefficients of D.16, the sum of the phases reproduces the whole-run measurement within 20 %, and the D.16 decision ladder names the rung that follows. ### MVP content

- **GIVEN** each of the nine MVP-FAULTS fixtures (O2-ROOM, O2-SHIP, PWR-OVL, CLT-LEAK, FUEL-DRIP, HULL-FATIGUE, SEAM-WEEP, BROKEN-BREAKER, PUMP-STALL — the catalogue above; with the footprint-driven BREACH-EVENT as a tenth), **WHEN** run headless with no player action, **THEN** each meets its D.15 row under that row's tolerance policy: absolute step counts to ±2 % for the single-mechanism rows — O2-ROOM ANOXIC 3,646, O2-SHIP pool empty 5,455, PWR-OVL DESTROYED 2,500 (±1 step, the tolerance of the PWR-OVL criterion above) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, PUMP-STALL SCRAM 350 steps after the stall; ±15 % of the integrated estimate for CLT-LEAK SCRAM 4,870 (± 730), FUEL-DRIP SCRAM 6,250 (± 940) and HULL-FATIGUE DECOMP 7,500 (± 1,125); BREACH-EVENT DECOMP 900–1,500; BROKEN-BREAKER first STARVE descriptor within 2 runs; SEAM-WEEP as its own criterion above. If true end-to-end halving of drain clocks is ever wanted it conflicts with LS-25 (the multipliers never touch supply) and goes to the user. - **GIVEN** V7 `top_up` is an MVP verb (user ruling X5) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*, **WHEN** Suit's top-up calls M16 `withdraw_store(node_id, amount)` on the F-MVP O2_TANK holding 1,500,000 mGU with `amount` 20,000, **THEN** granted = 20,000, the tank `stock` falls by exactly 20,000, the section's `withdrawn_mgu` rises by 20,000, the gas conservation invariant of LS-1 (e) holds on every run, and the call appears in no `power_use` field; **GIVEN** `amount` 2,000,000 on the same tank, **THEN** granted = 1,500,000 (clamped to stock, M16). The Graph needs no rule beyond M16, and no file under `src/sim/graph/` branches on `TOPUP_MODE`. (auto + lint) [LS-26; M16; Open Question 20 resolved]
- **GIVEN** `design/registry/entities.yaml` after this GDD's approval, **WHEN** read, **THEN** every ★ knob of Section G is present with the default stated there, and every constant this GDD cites from the registry (`TICK_RATE` 60, `MAX_DIRTY_KEYS` 256, `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4, `TILE_PX` 48, `rng_derive`, `state_hash`, `hook_runs_this_tick`) is unchanged. (review) [registry wording rule]

### Gaps / untestable as written

- **Retired 2026-10-27 (re-review, disagreement settled per G2):** the former criterion "each cross-GDD note of Open Question 33 is applied or answered in its owning GDD" is a process item tracked by `/consistency-check` and Open Questions 33 and 41, not a testable property of this GDD; a lint that greps for open-question ids proves nothing. Where a ruled value matters, a lint over the owning GDD greps for the value, as the Horror / Attention and Save/Load criteria above do. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*
- **GIVEN** the time-band targets of R21 are design intent, **WHEN** the MVP playtest reports that average players die without seeing phase B, **THEN** the Section G knobs are retuned and the D.15 table regenerated — "tense and absorbing for 30+ minutes" is the hypothesis, not a criterion this section can discharge. (manual) [D.15; MVP hypothesis]
- **GIVEN** the int-encoded dirty-key form `(KIND << 32) | id`, **WHEN** the kernel's dirty-key parameter type is confirmed, **THEN** either the int form is adopted or the string family form replaces it with no change to any rule — untestable until the kernel's type is read from its implementation. (review) [LS-20]
- **GIVEN** the VS-A levers (quiescence, stagger, compaction, Web Map deltas, fire, STRUT load), **WHEN** VS-A is planned, **THEN** each has its own criteria; none is testable at MVP and none is claimed here. (review) [LS-17, LS-26; D.16]
- **GIVEN** the R16 death consequence is decided (restart from the last autosave, 2026-10-27), **WHEN** `src/sim/graph/` is scanned, **THEN** nothing in it references a death, re-wake or autosave state or branches on one. (lint) [R16; Open Question 1, closed] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
